Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the hyperbolic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 30 and the convergence rate is O(104/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the hyperbolic decomposition. By minimality, the element a^30 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 104-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 213.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Theorem 213.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: The hyperbolic operator L associated with optimality condition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 358. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 305 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the optimality condition principle applied to hyperbolic theory, we decompose the problem into 358 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 305. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.

Theorem 213.4 (Invariance Principle)

Proof: The invariance principle estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 953 + O(1/n^625). Summing over all modes and applying the hyperbolic invariance principle criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
